### [86] [From SQL to Knowledge Graphs: An LLM-Driven Multi-Agent Approach with Data Schema Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26117)
*Dinh-Khanh Pham,Quy-Anh Dang,Lam Mai Thanh,Khanh Bui,Truong-Son Hy*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用LLM驱动的ETL代理和多智能体系统，将传统关系型数据库（RDBMS）自动转换为图谱数据库的端到端管道。系统通过ETL、分析器和图谱代理之间的循环讨论来迭代优化图谱schema设计，并在BFSI数据集上验证了图谱数据库比PostgreSQL在执行速度和问答准确率上均有显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RDBMS数据库存在多跳查询执行缓慢、缺乏图谱化可解释性等限制；现有RDBMS转换管道主要依赖传统加载命令和Cypher查询，但利用LLM生成有效图谱数据schema以减少歧义的研究仍属空白。

Method: 提出一种LLM驱动的ETL代理对表和列名进行标准化后存入Data Mart；采用多智能体系统（ETL、分析器、图谱代理）进行循环讨论，通过迭代建议与评分优化图谱数据库schema；最终需满足准确性、接地性（Groundedness）和忠实性（Faithfulness）三个标准后才进行数据转换。

Result: 在1,081个BFSI数据集样本上（分为易、中、难三个复杂度级别），CypherAgent在图谱数据库上实现85.6%的QA准确率，比SQLAgent在PostgreSQL上的准确率高12.12%；图谱数据库性能更快，延迟降低约3倍。

Conclusion: 该系统展示了将表格数据库自动转换为图谱数据库的高效端到端管道，证明LLM驱动的多智能体转换方法在准确性和执行效率上均优于传统RDBMS方案。

Abstract: RDBMS (Relational Database Management System) databases face several limitations, including slow execution with multi-hop queries and a lack of explainability by graphical interpretations. In contrast, Graph database offers a more intuitive and efficient data schema that performs faster execution on large datasets. Most existing RDBMS conversion pipelines focus on running traditional loading commands and relying on Cypher queries. However, the efficiency of using an LLM to generate an effective graph data schema, significantly reducing the ambiguity of the graph database, remains underexplored in the current research literature. This paper presents a novel algorithm that bridges RDBMS and graph database by using a novel LLM-powered ETL agent to standardize table and column names before saving them to the Data Mart. A Multi-Agent System generates a looping discussion between ETL, Analyzer, and Graph agents to optimize the final design through an iterative process of suggesting and scoring the graph database schema. We ensure that the final graph database meets three criteria before being accepted for data conversion: Accuracy, Groundedness, and Faithfulness. This system demonstrates an effective pipeline to automatically convert a tabular database into a graph database through a comprehensive end-to-end process. Our study highlights notable efficiency in using the converted graph database, which is measured on 1,081 samples of the BFSI dataset across three levels of complexity (easy, medium, and hard). Specifically, CypherAgent achieves an 85.6% accuracy for Q&A tasks using a Graph database, which is 12.12% higher than the accuracy achieved by an SQLAgent on the RDBMS database type PostgreSQL, for all queries. Additionally, the Graph database demonstrates faster performance, reducing latency by approximately 3 times.

</details>


### [87] [DRL: A Deterministic Relational Middleware Layer for Transaction-Safe Enterprise NL2SQL Under Schema-Graph Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26172)
*Sanjay Mishra,Divya Chukkapalli,Ganesh R. Naik*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出DRL（确定性关系中间件层）管道，通过动态上下文剪枝、关系AST类型化和事务性安全保障（EXPLAIN门控和NULL守卫）来解决企业OLTP目录上的自然语言接口扩展问题。在PostgreSQL和MySQL上评估，DRL实现92%的上下文缩减，中间件p95延迟仅4.6ms，并揭示了评估后处理器中的正则缺陷如何人为制造了4-10%的跨厂商性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业OLTP目录上的自然语言接口部署在大规模场景下失败，因为语义解析器在schema图扩展时会崩溃，导致上下文膨胀超出LLM注意力预算，现有方案缺乏系统性的上下文管理和安全验证机制。

Method: DRL管道包含三个核心组件：动态上下文剪枝（根据schema图规模动态压缩提示上下文）、关系AST类型化（确保生成查询的结构正确性）、事务性安全验证（EXPLAIN门控和NULL守卫来检测操作上的静默分歧SDop）。此外还构建了OLTP schema图扩展模型、1000对工作负载验证套件、B0-B3基线以及企业NL2SQL失败分类体系。

Result: DRL的schema链接提示（B1）相比朴素全目录提示（B0）实现76%上下文缩减；动态路由器（B2）达到92%缩减，剪枝p95为0.58ms，中间件p95为4.6ms。GPT-4o、Claude Sonnet 4.5和Gemini 2.5 Flash在修正评估基准下分别达到52.9%、52.8%和52.1%的执行匹配率；SDop标记了89-100%的假阳性EX通过查询。GPT-4o失败主要为语义/过滤错误（254/471），列幻觉仅占47/471。发现评估后处理器的单一正则缺陷压低了准确性并制造了虚假的4-10%跨厂商差距。

Conclusion: 企业NL2SQL应被视为系统工程问题——涉及上下文边界、验证和计划感知准入——而非排行榜竞赛。同时强调基准测试代码本身需要与所评估的模型一样受到严格审查。

Abstract: Deploying natural-language interfaces over enterprise OLTP catalogs fails at scale because semantic parsers collapse under schema-graph scaling, inflating context beyond stable LLM attention budgets. We present DRL (Deterministic Relational Middleware Layer), a safe pipeline interposing between front-ends and SQL backends. DRL comprises dynamic context pruning, relational AST typing, and transactional safeguard verification (EXPLAIN gating and NULL guards) to bound context and flag operational silent divergence (SDop). We evaluate DRL on PostgreSQL and MySQL, contributing (i) an OLTP schema-graph scaling model, (ii) a 1,000-pair Workload Verification Suite, (iii) baselines B0-B3, and (iv) an enterprise NL2SQL failure taxonomy. On PostgreSQL, schema-linked hints (B1) yield a 76% context reduction over naive full-catalog prompting (B0); DRL's dynamic router (B2) reaches a 92% reduction at pruning p95 = 0.58 ms and middleware p95 = 4.6 ms. GPT-4o, Claude Sonnet 4.5, and Gemini 2.5 Flash achieve 52.9%, 52.8%, and 52.1% execution match under a corrected evaluation harness; SDop flags 89-100% of false-positive EX-passing queries. GPT-4o failures are dominated by semantic/filter errors (254/471), while column hallucination is a minor factor (47/471). Crucially, a single regex defect in our evaluation post-processor silently suppressed accuracy and manufactured a false 4-10% cross-vendor gap that vanished when corrected, showing that benchmark code deserves the same scrutiny as the models it scores. DRL reframes enterprise NL2SQL as systems engineering - context bounding, verification, and plan-aware admission - not a leaderboard exercise.

</details>


### [88] [Realistic Counterfactual Explanations via Denial Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26335)
*Avia Asael,Nave Frost,Amir Gilad,Daniel Deutch*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出将可解释AI中的反事实解释与数据管理中的拒绝约束相结合，通过逻辑约束和数据清洗技术将反事实解释转化为符合真实数据分布的实例，在保证真实性的同时尽量减少对距离和多样性的妥协。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的反事实解释方法在追求距离最小化和多样性最大化时，往往会产生不符合真实数据分布的实例，这限制了反事实解释在实际场景中的可用性和实用性。

Method: 将可解释AI与数据管理思想结合：利用数据集中成立的拒绝约束来刻画反事实的真实性，并将可解释AI生成的反事实与数据清洗技术相结合，将不真实的反事实转化为真实的反事实，同时设计专用优化算法加速搜索过程。

Result: 在四个数据集上的大量实验验证了所提方法能够实现真实性，同时仅对距离和多样性造成相对较小的妥协；此外，针对反事实搜索的专用优化方法被证明非常有效。

Conclusion: 通过引入拒绝约束作为真实性约束，可以有效弥补现有反事实解释方法生成不真实实例的缺陷，且能够在真实性与距离、多样性之间取得良好的平衡，拓展了反事实解释的实用价值。

Abstract: In the realm of Explainable AI, classification results are often explained via counterfactuals (CFs for short), which are (ideally small) perturbations to an instance that lead to a change of classification label. Such CFs may serve as explanations for the prediction, pinpointing the features that were important. Existing explainability solutions typically aim at minimizing the distance of CFs from the original instance so that they are specific to it, and/or maximizing the diversity of CFs to cover multiple facets of the reasons underlying the prediction. In this paper, we note that in pursuing these aims, state-of-the-art explainability solutions may (and often do) yield counterfactual explanations that do not correspond to realistic instances. This limits their applicability and usefulness in practice. To remedy this, we combine ideas from Explainable AI with ideas from data management. Specifically, we capture realism of CFs via logical constraints that hold with respect to a dataset of examples (e.g., training set); the class of such constraints that we focus on is that of denial constraints, extensively studied in the context of relational databases. Algorithmically, we then combine explainable AI solutions to yield CFs, with ideas from data cleaning that we adapt to this unique setting, to transform CFs into realistic ones. Extensive experiments across four datasets validate that our solutions achieve realism with relatively minor compromise in terms of distance and diversity. They further validate that the dedicated optimizations that we have developed to speed up the search for CFs are indeed highly effective.

</details>


### [89] [VoS: Variate Ordering Strategies for Skyline Query Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26464)
*Abhinav Gorantla,Pratanu Mandal,K. Selçuk Candan,Maria Luisa Sapino*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出skyline算法应同时优化每元组支配检查和每属性支配检查，并论证属性排序对两者效率均有显著影响，通过策略寻找有效属性顺序以减少冗余比较，实验验证了该方法在标量和SIMD架构上的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统skyline算法优化集中于最小化元组对支配检查的总次数，但在实际操作中，两个元组之间的支配检查不一定需要对每个偏好属性进行评估，这导致支配检查优化与实际查询执行性能之间存在脱节。本文旨在弥合这一差距。

Method: 提出skyline算法需要同时优化每元组支配检查和每属性支配检查，基于这两个目标，提出若干策略来识别有效的属性（变量）排序，以减少冗余的属性比较。

Result: 在合成数据集和真实数据集上，以及在标量架构和SIMD架构上进行了大量实验，实验结果证实了所提方法在降低计算开销和提升skyline查询性能方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 属性排序对skyline计算效率有重要影响，通过优化属性顺序同时关注每元组和每属性的支配检查，可以显著提升skyline查询性能。

Abstract: Efficiency of skyline algorithms is highly influenced by the underlying data characteristics. Traditionally, optimization efforts have focused on minimizing the total number of tuple-pair dominance checks to improve query performance. However, in practice, a dominance check between two tuples does not necessarily require evaluating dominance relationships for each and every preference attribute of the data and this creates a disconnect between dominance checks optimization and query execution performance. In this paper, we argue that skyline algorithms need to optimize total per-attribute dominance checks, along with per-tuple dominance checks and that, for both of these goals, the ordering of the attributes (or variates) can have a substantial impact on the efficiency of skyline computation. Based on this premise, we present several strategies for identifying an effective variate order to minimize redundant attribute comparisons. Extensive experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets, and on both scalar and SIMD architectures, confirm the effectiveness of the proposed approach in reducing computational overhead and improving skyline query performance.

</details>


### [90] [IBLTs Measure Before They Decode: Self-Sizing Set Reconciliation from Pre-Peeling Counts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26537)
*Min Wu,Ji Qi,Chengdui Luo,Shudong Lu,Zhengsheng Ye,Zhengyang Wei*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于IBLT的自适应集合同步协议，通过从已接收的sketch中估计对称差基数d（无需额外传输），从而在一次失败轮次后精确确定第二轮的容量，实现两轮内完成同步并给出成功概率保证。该方案在NineData Cloud的90天、41,603次运行中进行验证，并在Redis和Pika上部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集合同步中，标准IBLT的容量需要与未知的差异基数d匹配，但实际数据库场景中d跨越多个数量级。容量不足导致重新扫描或重放，容量过大浪费网络和内存资源，因此需要一种无需额外通信即可估计d的方法。

Method: 利用IBLT每个cell的计数数组构造二次统计量，得出d的无偏估计量；为标准IBLT推导精确的均值和方差公式及卡方置信区间；通过映射感知定理将构造扩展到Irregular、Rateless和MET IBLT变体；设计两轮自定大小协议，第一轮失败后一步计算出第二轮容量。

Result: 在NineData Cloud的41,603次同步运行（90天）中验证了方法的有效性，与Merkle式定位的跨引擎重放进行了端到端对比，并在中国移动的Redis和Pika上成功部署。

Conclusion: 第一轮同步失败并非浪费，反而产出了已经"付费"的差异基数测量值，使得第二轮容量精确匹配真实差异规模，在不增加额外通信的前提下实现了高效的自适应集合同步。

Abstract: Set reconciliation recovers the symmetric difference $A\triangle B$ with communication far below the data volume. Invertible Bloom Lookup Tables (IBLTs) are a standard tool, but their capacity must match the unknown difference cardinality~$d$. In database deployments, $d$ spans orders of magnitude, so undersizing causes rescans or replays while oversizing wastes network and memory.
  We show that an IBLT measures $d$ before decoding. Each cell count records how many difference elements map to it; a quadratic statistic over the count array gives an exactly unbiased estimate with no extra transmitted bytes. The estimate remains valid after decoding failure because it is read from the same sketch. For standard IBLTs, we derive exact mean and variance formulas and chi-square confidence intervals. The same chi-square law governs failed instances in deep overload. A mapping-aware theorem extends the construction to Irregular, Rateless, and MET IBLTs through per-variant adapters.
  Our self-sizing protocol uses a first-round failure to compute the second-round capacity in one step, completing within two interaction rounds with an explicit success-probability guarantee. We profile 41{,}603 reconciliation runs over 90 days at NineData Cloud, compare end-to-end against Merkle-style localization in a cross-engine replay of production tables, and deploy across Redis and Pika in China Mobile. The first round therefore turns an unknown difference cardinality into a measurement it has already paid for.

</details>


### [91] [Size Bounds for CQs Under Acyclic Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26775)
*Stefan Mengel,Andrei Romashchenko*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文研究了连接查询（CQ）结果大小界限，比较了渐近紧但不可计算的熵界与其可计算的松弛——多胞形界。在无投影的循环函数依赖（AFD）下两者一致，但引入投影后即使存在AFD，两界之间也会出现多项式差距；同时给出了一个多胞形界紧的特殊情况，其由输出变量在查询变量拓扑序中的位置来刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连接查询结果大小界限在数据库理论中扮演关键角色。熵界渐近紧但不可计算，多胞形界可计算但一般不紧。已有研究表明在无投影的循环函数依赖下两者一致，但投影对两者关系的影响尚不清楚，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 通过理论分析对比熵界与多胞形界在循环函数依赖及投影条件下的大小关系。首先展示在允许投影的一般情况下两界存在多项式差距的构造性证明；其次通过输出变量在查询变量拓扑序中的位置这一条件，刻画多胞形界变紧的特殊情形。

Result: 研究发现：当允许投影时，即使在循环函数依赖条件下，熵界与多胞形界之间普遍存在多项式差距；然而，在一个由输出变量在拓扑序中的位置所刻画的特殊情况下，多胞形界可以被证明是紧的。

Conclusion: 投影的存在破坏了循环函数依赖下熵界与多胞形界的一致性，揭示了投影对查询结果大小界限的显著影响；同时所发现的多胞形界紧的特殊情况为更精确的可计算界限提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We study size bounds for conjunctive query (CQ) results which in recent years have played a crucial role in database theory. In particular, we compare the so-called entropic bound which is known to be asymptotically tight but not known to be computable, and its computable relaxation called the polymatroid bound, which is generally not tight. We focus here on conjunctive queries under acyclic functional dependencies. These queries are known to be well-behaved in the sense that, in the case without projections, both bounds coincide. We show that this picture changes when projections are allowed: in this case, even for acyclic functional dependencies, there is in general a polynomial gap between the two bounds. We complement this negative result by showing a special case for which the polymatroid bound is tight under acyclic functional dependencies and projections that is characterized by the position of the output variables in a topological order of the query variables.

</details>


### [92] [Incremental Delta-Shapley: A Standalone Runtime for Predicate Attribution on Sliding Windows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26930)
*Pouya Khani,Ira Assent*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出IDS（Incremental Delta-Shapley）系统，将谓词级Shapley归因的闭式解转化为可部署的实时分析解释引擎，支持滑动窗口上的增量维护、未注册谓词查询和自适应提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有工作解决了谓词级Shapley归因的数学闭式解，但如何在实际运行时中维护摘要、暴露归因、应答未注册谓词以及摊销重复的ad hoc查询仍未被解决，需要将这些理论成果落地为可部署系统。

Method: IDS是一个单节点运行时系统，消费窗口维护增量并更新全局、边际和原子摘要，以常数时间评估任何闭式形式；重叠谓词使用原子细化；提供受限SQL风格API暴露归因及其滑动变化；未注册谓词通过保留状态扫描、倒排索引或带浓度保证的摊销滑动窗口采样应答，频繁谓词通过重建细化进行提升。

Result: 在合成、对抗、NEXMark风格和纽约出租车工作负载上，归因结果与穷举Shapley枚举在浮点精度内匹配；增量维护在N上保持平坦，比逐窗口扫描相同形式快最多4.3×10^5倍；自适应提升在Zipfian轨迹上将ad hoc成本降低最多9.2倍。

Conclusion: IDS成功将谓词级Shapley归因的理论闭式解工程化为高性能、可部署的解释系统，兼具准确性、效率和灵活性。

Abstract: Continuous aggregate queries over sliding windows are common in real-time analytics, but most systems report \emph{what} an aggregate is doing without attributing \emph{which} predicates account for the result. A companion paper~\cite{khani2026closedformpredicatelevelshapleyattribution} shows that exact predicate-level Shapley attribution for SUM, COUNT, AVG, and variance needs only three additive predicate summaries with closed-form coefficients. Those results settle the mathematics, not how a runtime maintains summaries across slides, exposes attribution, answers unregistered predicates, or amortizes repeated ad hoc ones.
  We present \textbf{IDS} (Incremental Delta-Shapley), a standalone single-node runtime that turns those closed forms into a deployable explanation system. IDS consumes window-maintenance deltas, updates global, marginal, and atom summaries, and evaluates any closed form in constant time. Overlapping predicates use atomic refinement, and a restricted SQL-like API exposes attribution and its per-slide change as first-class operators. Unregistered predicates are answered by a retained-state scan, an inverted index, or an amortized sliding-window sample with concentration guarantees; frequent ones are promoted by rebuilding the refinement. On synthetic, adversarial, NEXMark-style, and NYC taxi workloads, attribution matches exhaustive Shapley enumeration to floating-point precision; incremental maintenance is flat in $N$ and up to $4.3\times10^{5}\times$ faster than per-window scans of the same form; and adaptive promotion cuts ad hoc cost by up to $9.2\times$ on Zipfian traces.

</details>


### [93] [Compositional Online Learning for Semantic Data Processing Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27244)
*Paweł Liskowski,Fuheng Zhao,Benjamin Han,Anupam Datta,Dimitris Tsirogiannis*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在LLM调用边界进行组合式在线学习的框架，用于语义数据处理系统。由于LLM调用延迟高、成本高，其往返时间足以隐藏CPU端学习器的更新。框架沿决策粒度和学习器更新节奏两个维度展开，并包含记忆化层、在线过滤排序学习器和在线级联路由学习器三个组件。在Cortex AISQL生产环境中，组合学习组件在代表性合取过滤工作负载上实现了约8倍的查询成本加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在语义数据处理系统中，LLM调用占据了查询成本的80-90%，每次调用比关系谓词贵10^5-10^7倍。这颠覆了经典自适应查询处理的设计约束——传统上在线学习器必须保持轻量以避免主导被优化的谓词开销，而LLM的延迟窗口恰好允许更重的学习组件在往返时间内完成更新，因此需要新的学习框架来利用这一反转后的设计空间。

Method: 提出组合式在线学习框架：1) 沿决策粒度（每调用vs每批次）和学习器更新节奏两个维度定义设计空间；2) 所有组件共享同一学习模式——将每个训练器步骤隐藏在下一个LLM往返之内；3) 在Cortex AISQL中组合三个组件：记忆化层、在线每调用过滤排序学习器、在线每批次级联路由学习器；4) 通过条件成本分解将每个学习组件分配给每行LLM成本的不同因子。

Result: 在独立性假设下，两个学习组件组合可乘法地达到代表性合取过滤工作负载的11.4倍成本上限；考虑级联边界的自选择、样本预算缩减和选择性估计漂移等实际因素后，实际加速比约为8倍。

Conclusion: LLM调用的高延迟改变了自适应查询处理的设计约束，使得在LLM往返时间内进行在线学习成为可行且高效的方法。组合式在线学习框架能够利用LLM调用边界的延迟窗口，实现在生产环境语义数据处理系统中显著的查询成本优化。

Abstract: An LLM call in a semantic data processing system is expensive enough to dominate query cost, yet slow enough to hide a CPU-side learner's update behind its round-trip. In production, LLM compute accounts for $80-90\%$ of query cost, and each call costs $10^5-10^7\times$ a relational predicate. The latency window inverts a design constraint of classical adaptive query processing, where online learners had to stay lightweight to avoid dominating the predicates they optimize. At LLM latency, per-call gradient steps and per-batch threshold solves fit inside the round-trip. We develop compositional online learning at the LLM call boundary: a framework for combining online-learning components in semantic data processing systems. Each component makes execution-time decisions and refines its learned artifacts online. The design space spans two axes, decision granularity and learner update cadence, and the components share a single learning pattern that hides each trainer step inside the next LLM round-trip. A production case study in Cortex AISQL composes three components: a memoization layer, an online per-call filter-ordering learner, and an online per-batch cascade-routing learner. A conditional cost decomposition assigns each learning component to a distinct factor of per-row LLM cost. Under independence, the two learning components compose multiplicatively to an $11.4\times$ upper bound on a representative conjunction-filter workload. Self-selection at the cascade boundary, sample-budget shrinkage, and selectivity-estimation drift reduce it to a realistic figure near $8\times$.

</details>

### [94] [Emotion Understanding in Streaming Video with Trajectory-Aware Reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26786)
*Qingsong Wang,Qigong Lei,Zitong Wang,Bohan Yu,Zhiang Dong,Jian liu,Weiqiang Wang,Chang Yao,Jingyuan Chen*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出TRACE框架，用于流式视频情感理解，通过轨迹感知的可靠性评估（置信度、熵、稳定性、类切换模式），在低延迟在线路径与多模态上下文推理路径之间动态选择，实现准确性与计算成本的优化平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频情感理解大多作为离线分类问题处理，要求完整视频可用。然而实时交互场景需要从不完整且不断演化的证据中做出情感决策，且单一置信的早期前缀预测可能因信念轨迹不稳定或类别反复切换而不可靠，因此需要可靠性感知的流式决策框架。

Method: TRACE框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 从流式音频前缀生成低延迟情感信念；(2) 基于置信度、熵、稳定性及类切换模式估计可靠性；(3) 对于不确定案例，选择性调用包含视觉、文本及相邻话语上下文的多模态信念重解释。稳定案例走低延迟在线路径，模糊案例分配更强多模态推理。

Result: 在StreamMER、MELD和MER2024数据集上的实验表明，TRACE改善了准确性-成本权衡，保留了大部分全上下文推理收益，同时减少了不必要的上下文推理计算。

Conclusion: TRACE验证了轨迹感知的可靠性评估在流式情感理解中的有效性，通过选择性多模态推理实现了低延迟与高准确率之间的最优折中，为实时情感交互系统提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Video emotion understanding is commonly studied as an offline classification problem, where the complete video segment is available before prediction. Real-time interaction, however, requires emotion decisions from incomplete and evolving evidence. This paper studies streaming video emotion understanding as a reliability-aware decision process over evolving emotion beliefs. In this setting, a single confident prefix prediction can still be unreliable when the underlying belief trajectory is unstable or repeatedly switches across emotion classes. We propose TRACE, a trajectory-aware reliability framework that forms low-latency emotion beliefs from streaming audio prefixes, estimates reliability from confidence, entropy, stability, and class-switching patterns, and selectively invokes contextual belief reinterpretation with visual, textual, and neighboring-utterance evidence. TRACE keeps stable cases in the low-latency online pathway while allocating stronger multimodal reasoning to uncertain cases that remain ambiguous. Experiments on StreamMER, MELD, and MER2024 show that TRACE improves the accuracy-cost trade-off, retaining most full-context gains while reducing unnecessary contextual reasoning.

</details>


### [95] [Self-Reflective Multi-modal Reasoning for Short-Video Fake News Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26787)
*Pinjie Xu,Yuzhou Yang,Zhikai Tan,Qichao Ying,Zaiyang Yu,Ce Li,Zhenxing Qian*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出SRM-FND，一个面向短视频假新闻检测的自我反思多模态推理框架。它通过对比深思、迭代根因诊断和修正性提示精炼来提升推理质量，并结合盲分析师、反结论推理器和自洽仲裁器三种角色协作识别有效判别依据。框架还引入双阶段主题自适应视觉-语言模型微调，以及针对不确定案例的置信度驱动的跨样本复核机制。在FakeSV和FakeTT数据集上的实验表明，SRM-FND优于强基线，预测更可靠可解释，跨数据集性能也有显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有假新闻检测管线虽越来越多地利用大语言模型和视觉-语言模型进行基于推理的分析，但仍面临三大挑战：一是缺乏真值思维链监督下如何通过自我反思提升推理质量；二是如何将改进的推理用于下游模型微调；三是如何将单样本欺诈模式发现与跨样本验证相连接。

Method: SRM-FND框架包含三个核心模块：（1）自我反思推理机制：通过对比深思、迭代根因诊断和修正性提示精炼生成更高质量的推理，由盲分析师、反结论推理器和自洽仲裁器三种角色协作，共同识别和保留有判别力的推理依据；（2）双阶段主题自适应视觉-语言模型微调：提升多模态基础能力并支持轻量级主题特化；（3）置信度驱动的跨样本复核：对不确定案例检索可信与可疑的同事件样本进行交叉验证。

Result: 在FakeSV和FakeTT两个数据集上的实验显示，SRM-FND在检测性能上优于强基线模型，生成的预测更加可靠且具有更好的可解释性，同时在跨数据集泛化性能上取得了显著的提升。

Conclusion: SRM-FND通过将自我反思推理、多模态微调和跨样本验证有机结合，有效解决了短视频假新闻检测中推理质量提升、推理与微调的衔接以及单样本与跨样本发现之间的连接等关键问题，证明了该框架在准确性和可解释性上的优势。

Abstract: Recent fake news detection pipelines increasingly leverage large language models and vision-language models for reasoning-based analysis. However, several challenges remain open: improving reasoning quality through self-reflection without ground-truth chain-of-thought supervision, using improved reasoning to benefit downstream model fine-tuning, and connecting single-sample fraudulent-pattern discovery with cross-sample verification. We propose SRM-FND, a self-reflective multimodal reasoning framework for short-video fake news detection. SRM-FND develops higher-quality reasoning through contrastive deliberation, iterative root-cause diagnosis, and corrective prompt refinement. A Blind Analyst, Counter-Conclusion Reasoner, and Self-Consistency Arbiter collaboratively identify and retain discriminative rationales. The framework also incorporates dual-phase, topic-adaptive vision-language model fine-tuning to improve multimodal grounding and enable lightweight topic specialization. For uncertain cases, it performs confidence-driven cross-sample review by retrieving credible and suspicious co-event examples. Experiments on FakeSV and FakeTT show that SRM-FND outperforms strong baselines, produces more reliable and interpretable predictions, and delivers noticeable improvements in cross-dataset performance.

</details>


### [96] [How AI Experiences Art: Emergent Aesthetic Structure in a Self-Supervised Multimodal Embedding Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27121)
*Corey D. C. Heath*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个自监督框架，将文本、音频、图像和视频四种模态投影到共享的256维嵌入空间，并通过迭代聚类来发现美学结构，探讨了AI聚类结果与人类情感标签之间的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 美学是艺术作品象征意义的重要组成部分，但AI模型如何在无显式标签或跨模态监督的情况下，自主形成对人类媒介的美学分类，这一方向尚未被充分探索。

Method: 采用自监督框架，将四种模态（文本、音频、图像和视频）映射到共享的256维嵌入空间，并应用迭代聚类算法来发现美学结构，进而在弱监督多模态数据集上比较AI聚类与人类情感标注之间的分歧。

Result: 揭示了AI生成的聚类分配与人类情感标注之间的差异，展示了AI自主形成的跨模态美学结构。

Conclusion: 该工作有助于理解AI如何构建跨模态相似性，可用于异构媒体集合的组织（服务于RAG）以及自动化数据标注。

Abstract: Aesthetics are an important part of the symbolism of artistic works. Although subjective, humans categorize art based on the emotion evoked regardless of modality. What remains under-explored is how AI models form their own aesthetic categorization of human-produced media without explicit labels or cross-modal supervision. We present a self-supervised framework that projects four modalities (text, audio, image and video) into a shared 256-dimensional embedding space and applies iterative clustering to discover aesthetic structure. We discuss the divergence between AI-generated cluster assignments and human affective register labels on a weakly supervised multimodal dataset. This work has applications in understanding how AI structures cross-modal similarity, organizing heterogeneous media collections for Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG), and automated data labeling.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [97] [Agentic AI Containment Architecture for Security Hardening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26108)
*Mohamed ElBendary*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Agent Containment Architecture，将多智能体系统安全作为架构属性而非事后附加层，通过六个约束条件实现可机器验证的安全契约，涵盖职责分离、部署前一致性检查、价值流绑定、时间隔离、知识验证和确定性完整性校验，并采用Propose-Verify-Act-Verify执行模型来防御提示注入、编排器操纵等关键威胁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体AI系统在自主协调、工具使用和持续学习方面面临新型安全与治理风险，但现有遏制（containment）方法不成熟，主要依赖设计后甚至部署后的附加安全层，缺乏将安全作为架构属性从源头嵌入的方法论。

Method: 提出Agent Containment Architecture，包含形式化的组织映射（从系统分析工件到机器可验证契约）和六大交互约束：职责分离、部署前一致性检查、价值流绑定、时间隔离、严格知识验证、结构与流程完整性确定性验证。这些约束共同实现Propose-Verify-Act-Verify执行模型，并通过命题和正确性推理论证其防御效果。

Result: 论文给出了约束与关键威胁类别（提示注入、编排器操纵、跨会话状态投毒、涌现式智能体共谋）之间的防御关联论证，并通过简历筛选案例研究展示架构在对抗条件下产生可审计、合规的结果。同时将结构完整性与语义安全性明确分离，使残余语义风险显式化且可度量。

Conclusion: 该架构将安全从事后的补丁式附加转变为架构级的设计约束，通过合同化定义和独立验证确保所有操作可追溯，虽不能完全消除语义风险，但能将其边界化并使残余风险显式和可测量，为多智能体系统安全治理提供了系统化的架构方案。

Abstract: Multi-agent AI systems are increasingly deployed in contexts where autonomous coordination, tool use, and continuous learning introduce novel security and governance risks. The containment approach to multi-agent system security remains underdeveloped, primarily resorting to add-on layers post-design and sometimes post-implementation and deployment. This paper proposes an Agent Containment Architecture that treats security as an architectural property enforced through a set of explicit constraints that bound the design space of multi-agent systems. The architecture proposed in this paper introduces a novel formal organizational mapping from standard systems analysis artifacts to machine-verifiable contracts under a proposed constraint system. The architecture introduces six interacting constraints: separation of responsibility assignments, pre-deployment coherence checking, value stream binding, temporal isolation, strict knowledge verification before accumulation, and deterministic verification of structural and process integrity. Together, these constraints enforce a Propose-Verify-Act-Verify execution model in which all operations are contractually defined, independently verified, and traceable to specific execution contexts. The paper presents propositions and correctness reasoning arguments linking these constraints to defenses against key threat classes, including prompt injection, orchestrator manipulation, cross-session state poisoning, and emergent agent collusion. A resume screening case study demonstrates how the architecture produces auditable, policy-compliant outcomes under adversarial conditions. The work explicitly separates structural integrity from semantic safety, bounding residual risks while making residual semantic risk explicit and measurable.

</details>


### [98] [Four Ways to Forge a Bundle My Own Verifier Calls Clean: Refusal-Site Mutation Testing of an Evidence-Bundle Verifier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26183)
*Erik Hill*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 作者构建了一个允许陌生人离线重跑验证声明的协议，但外部审计发现其验证器存在"空转通过"（vacuous pass）缺陷——检查路径未实际检查任何内容就报告成功。修复后作者自测发现同类缺陷仍存在多次，并通过系统化测量构建了拒绝点（refusal site）语料库进行量化评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者最初旨在构建一个可离线重复验证的协议，但在外部审计被攻破后，发现核心缺陷是一种普遍存在的验证器逻辑漏洞：检查在未检查任何东西的路径上报告成功（vacuous pass）。作者认为这并非个例，因此决定停止收集轶事，转而进行系统性测量，以量化该缺陷在验证器中的分布与影响。

Method: 作者在固定提交点（f59fb62）对验证器进行系统化审计，识别出112个拒绝点（refusal site）；通过删除测试与篡改夹具（tamper fixture）的绿色通过实验来评估每个拒绝点的必要性；构建了16个夹具的语料库来测试验证器的拒绝能力；最终在146个拒绝点上用全部11次测量序列（而非仅5行汇总表）报告遮蔽率与被测量系列的非单调性。

Result: 在f59fb62处，112个拒绝点中有75个可被删除而所有测试仍保持绿色，得分为0.330。四个手工发现的伪造中三个属于幸存类别，第四个是无拒绝点的义务。16夹具语料库单独得分10（0.625→0.941→1.000），在92e4548处增长到146个拒绝点。修复四个发现缺陷仅将37/112提升到39/119。测量工具自身有7次空转通过，其中1次返回完美1.000。

Conclusion: 作者坦诚指出所有数据均来自自建的封闭系统，唯一的独立数据点是外部审计。这种自我测量的局限性不是被掩盖的缺陷，而是论文可信度的组成部分——作者明确将其前置陈述，而非作为事后免责声明。这体现了对验证验证器本身这一元问题的诚实处理。

Abstract: I built a protocol whose premise is that a stranger can re-run my claims offline and get the same answer. An outside engineer audited it and broke it: a bundle whose headline numbers were false verified clean, the cheapest forgery four bytes. I merged his fix, then pointed my own instruments at the fixed verifier and found the same defect four more times, in places his audit did not reach. The cheapest is one capital letter.
  The unifying defect is not cryptographic or exotic: a check that reports success along a path where it never examined anything. Vacuous pass is a working label, not a discovery; Section 4 names the literatures already occupying it.
  So I stopped collecting anecdotes and measured. At f59fb62, under the extraction rule of Section 6, the verifier exposes 112 refusal sites; 75 could be deleted with the whole suite and every tamper fixture still green, a score of 0.330. Three of the four hand-found forgeries fall in surviving classes; the fourth is an obligation with no refusal site. Scored alone, the sixteen-fixture corpus built to prove the verifier can refuse catches 10. Testing the refusals themselves took it to 0.941, then to 1.000 at 92e4548 over a grown population of 146 sites; those denominators differ and the series between them is non-monotone, so Section 7.5 carries all eleven, not just the five rows of Table 2. Fixing the four found defects instead moved 37/112 to 39/119, leaving the pre-existing sites at 37.
  Seven times during this study my own measuring tools reported success while measuring nothing; four were built to detect this class, and one returned a perfect 1.000.
  Every number here is self-measured on a system I wrote, over a registry that is a closed loop of my own repositories; the one external data point is the audit of Section 2.2. That is stated here rather than buried: it is the paper's credibility, not a caveat.

</details>


### [99] [Cost-Utility Alignment in LLM Agent Trajectories:Profiling,Attribution,Diagnosis,Adaptation,and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26195)
*Dan Liu,Jian Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种以轨迹为中心的"成本-效用对齐"分析框架，将智能体执行过程中的资源消耗与任务贡献视为同一执行过程的双重账本，通过五个分析阶段（成本剖析、效用归因、错位诊断、定向适应、评估）构建闭环分析，弥补了现有调查中将推理优化、智能体能力与评估割裂处理的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体通过多步轨迹执行任务，逐步累积令牌、延迟、货币费用和环境风险等成本，但传统调查仅孤立地研究推理优化、智能体能力或评估，缺乏原理性工具来判断轨迹的资源消耗是否与任务贡献相匹配。

Method: 提出轨迹中心的成本-效用对齐框架，将资源消耗与任务贡献视为同一执行的双重账本，围绕五个分析阶段组织：成本剖析、效用归因、错位诊断、定向适应和评估。核心是效用归因环节，按证据强度组织贡献方法（从过程代理、信息依赖到反事实重放），提供因果证据支持诊断和适应；覆盖五种错位形式：认知与上下文使用、外部交互、恢复循环控制、资源-能力分配、多智能体协调。

Result: 利用该框架分析近期智能体系统、归因方法和评估协议（涵盖效率、可靠性和经济价值），识别五种错位形态及其定向适应方案，最终形成连接成本侧与效用侧的闭环分析回路，为资源感知的智能体设计与部署提供结构化基础。

Conclusion: 该框架为从业者提供了系统化的原则性工具，使其能够在智能体设计与部署中同时权衡资源消耗与任务贡献，弥补了现有研究与实践中"成本"与"效用"脱节的空白。

Abstract: LLM agents execute tasks through multi-step trajectories that accumulate cost in tokens, latency, monetary fees, and environmental risk while producing utility only at the aggregate task level. Prior surveys address inference optimization, agent capabilities, or evaluation in isolation, leaving practitioners without principled tools to determine whether a trajectory's resource expenditure is justified by its task contribution. We address this gap by developing a trajectory-centric cost-utility alignment framework that treats resource consumption and task contribution as dual ledgers over the same execution, organized around five analytical stages: cost profiling, utility attribution, misalignment diagnosis, targeted adaptation, and evaluation. Utility attribution is central to this structure: rather than relying on aggregate outcomes, it organizes contribution methods by evidential strength, from process proxies and information dependency to counterfactual replay, supplying the causal evidence that grounds diagnosis and guides adaptation. Using this framework, we analyze recent agent systems, attribution methods, and evaluation protocols covering efficiency, reliability, and economic value, as well as five forms of misalignment spanning cognitive and context use, external interaction, recovery-loop control, resource-capability allocation, and multi-agent coordination, together with their targeted adaptations. The result is a closed analytical loop connecting the cost side of agent execution to its utility side, providing a structured basis for resource-aware agent design and deployment.

</details>


### [100] [Harness Engineering for Predictable Agentic Systems: An Empirical Study of Deterministic Execution Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26197)
*Saransh Dhage*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM智能体的"执行框架工程"（harness engineering），通过为智能体添加确定性执行层（有限状态控制、强制工具选择、输出验证、有界重试、结构化规划），在金融和法律的合成任务上测试其对执行确定性和任务成功率的影响。研究发现：初步框架效果不一致，但加入结构化规划后，四个模型-任务组合中有三个达到100%的可复现率和任务成功率，代价是延迟因模型而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体即使在相同任务和工具下也有显著的运行间执行方差，这在探索性使用中可接受，但在金融、合规等受监管领域不可接受，因此需要系统性地研究如何提高执行确定性。

Method: 采用"执行框架工程"方法：为智能体包装确定性执行层，包括有限状态控制、强制工具选择、输出验证、有界重试和结构化规划。使用两个合成任务（金融、法律）和两个开源模型（Qwen-2.5-7B-Instruct、Gemma-3-27B）进行实验，通过可复现率和确定性指数量化评估，并进行轨迹级诊断定位方差来源。

Result: 初步框架仅在四个模型-任务组合中的一个显著提升可复现性，在两个中显著降低，在一个中无效果。轨迹级诊断发现自由文本规划步骤是剩余方差的主要来源。加入结构化规划（在调用工具前按固定模式验证计划）后，四个组合中三个达到可复现率和确定性指数1.000（N=100），任务成功率在三个组合中升至100%。令牌成本在所有组合中均下降，但延迟按模型呈现显著分化——一个模型加速，另一个明显变慢。

Conclusion: 执行框架工程是提升智能体可靠性的独特且有效的学科方法，但其成本是模型依赖的，必须通过实际测量而非假设来确定。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) based agents exhibit substantial run-to-run execution variance even when given identical tasks and tools -- acceptable for exploratory use but unacceptable in regulated domains such as finance and compliance. We study harness engineering: wrapping an agent in a deterministic execution layer (finite-state control, forced tool selection, output validation, bounded retry, and structured planning) and measuring its effect on execution determinism and task success. Across two synthetic tasks (finance and legal) and two open-weight models (Qwen-2.5-7B-Instruct, Gemma-3-27B), a first-pass harness produces a mixed result: it significantly improves reproducibility in one of four model-task cells, significantly degrades it in two, and has no effect in the fourth. A trace-level diagnostic finds the cause: once tool sequence, state sequence, and output are already highly consistent, an unconstrained free-text planning step becomes the dominant remaining source of variance. Adding Structured Planning -- validating the plan against a fixed schema before any tool is invoked -- eliminates the effect entirely: three of four cells reach a Reproducibility Rate and Determinism Index of 1.000 at N=100, and task success rises to 100% in three of four cells. The gain is not free: token cost falls in every cell, but latency shows a genuine, sample-size-robust split by model -- one becomes faster under the constraint, the other markedly slower. We argue harness engineering is a distinct, effective discipline for agent reliability, but its cost is model-dependent and must be measured, not assumed.

</details>


### [101] [Fairness Invariants: A Relational Approach to Explaining and Mitigating Fairness Bugs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26209)
*Ranit Debnath Akash,Ashish Kumar,Gang Tan,Saeid Tizpaz-Niari*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出REMI框架，用于自动化定位、解释和缓解个体歧视问题。该方法受形式化方法中循环不变式合成的启发，将反事实公平性视为关系不变式发现问题，通过双向关系解释框架和三种数据对齐技术推断可解释规则模型作为"公平不变式"，在不重新训练模型的情况下阻挡或重新标记不公平预测。实验表明REMI在83%以上的案例中定位到真实公平性缺陷，并将黑盒模型中的歧视性决策降低最多70%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注检测和量化个体歧视问题，但缺乏系统化的机制来解释和定位公平性缺陷。当前的解释技术大多针对单输入决策设计，无法处理歧视本质上的关系性特征——即涉及原始样本与反事实样本之间的比较。

Method: REMI框架借鉴形式化方法中的循环不变式合成思想，将反事实公平性建模为关系不变式发现问题。采用双向关系解释框架，在配对样本(x, x')上学习以识别公平性被违反的输入空间区域；与传统单向蕴含不变式推断不同，REMI强制执行双向约束，要求在原始样本和反事实样本上输出结果一致。REMI利用三种数据对齐技术推断可解释的基于规则的模型作为"公平不变式"，这些规则作为防护栏用于选择性地阻挡或重新标记不公平预测，无需模型重训练。

Result: 在符号程序和神经网络程序上的评估表明，REMI在83%以上的案例中成功定位到真实公平性缺陷，显著优于现有最先进基线方法，并在黑盒模型中将歧视性决策减少高达70%。

Conclusion: REMI为个体公平性缺陷提供了首个系统化的定位、解释和缓解框架，通过关系不变式的视角填补了该领域解释与定位机制缺失的空白，验证了形式化方法思想在机器学习公平性领域的有效迁移。

Abstract: Data-driven software systems are increasingly deployed in high-stakes socio-economic domains, from criminal justice to financial lending. However, these systems often exhibit individual discrimination---unjustified disparities in which a program yields different outcomes for similar individuals who differ only in their protected attributes (e.g., race, gender, age). While existing research has focused on detecting and quantifying these bugs, there remains a critical lack of principled mechanisms to explain and localize individual fairness bugs. Current explanation techniques are largely designed for single-input decisions rather than the relational nature of discrimination, which inherently involves a comparison between an original and a counterfactual pair.
  We present REMI, a framework for the automated localization, explanation, and mitigation of individual discrimination. Inspired by loop-invariant synthesis in formal methods, we treat counterfactual fairness as a relational invariant discovery problem. We introduce a bidirectional relational explanation framework that learns over paired examples $(x, x')$ to identify regions of the input space where fairness is violated. Unlike traditional one-way implication pairs used in invariant inference, our approach enforces bidirectional constraints: requiring identical outcomes for both original and counterfactual samples. REMI utilizes three data-alignment techniques to infer interpretable rule-based models that act as "fairness invariants." These rules serve as guardrails to selectively block or relabel unfair predictions without requiring model retraining. Our evaluation on symbolic and neural network programs demonstrates that REMI localizes ground-truth fairness bugs in over 83% of cases, significantly outperforming state-of-the-art baselines and reducing discriminatory decisions in black-box models by up to 70%.

</details>


### [102] [Characterizing the Landscape of Open-Source Satellite Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26211)
*Jinfeng Wen,Qi Liang,Yuehan Sun,Federica Sarro,Ao Zhou,Xuanzhe Liu,Shangguang Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次对开源卫星软件进行系统性特征化研究，挖掘并分析了22,286个GitHub卫星相关项目，围绕流行趋势、软件目标和开发实践三个研究问题展开，构建了涵盖43个软件目标类别的分类体系，揭示了高度异构且任务专业化的卫星软件生态系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卫星已成为现代技术系统的关键组成部分，支撑通信、导航、地球观测和科学研究等关键基础设施。随着太空探索的推进和卫星服务需求的增长，对复杂异构卫星软件的依赖日益增加，需要对卫星软件全景进行系统理解，但现有研究缺乏全面的实证考察。

Method: 挖掘并分析了22,286个卫星相关的GitHub项目，通过三个研究问题展开：（1）RQI：分析项目和活跃开发者的时间演化趋势；（2）RQ2：人工审查646个项目，构建包含43个软件目标类别的分类体系，涵盖概念设计、数据集、系统实现、仿真、测试和工具等领域；（3）RQ3：对含源代码的项目进行深入分析，考察编程语言多样性和实现策略。

Result: 研究发现卫星软件项目数量和活跃开发者持续增长，呈现出日益流行的趋势；通过人工审查646个项目构建了43个软件目标类别的分类体系；源代码分析揭示了高度异构和任务专业化的生态系统，涉及66种编程语言和多样化的实现策略。

Conclusion: 文章总结了关键发现，并为卫星开发者和研究人员提供了可操作的建议，填补了开源卫星软件领域缺乏全面实证研究的空白。

Abstract: Satellites have become fundamental components of modern technological systems, supporting critical infrastructure in communication, navigation, Earth observation, and scientific research. As space exploration advances and demand for satellite-enabled services grows, reliance on complex, heterogeneous satellite software continues to increase. A systematic understanding of the satellite software landscape is therefore increasingly important, yet existing studies still lack a comprehensive empirical examination. To address this gap, we present the first characterization study of open-source satellite software, examining its ecosystem and development practices. We mine and analyze 22,286 satellite-related GitHub projects through three research questions on popularity trends (RQ1), software goals (RQ2), and development practices (RQ3). First, we characterize the temporal evolution of projects and active developers, revealing increasing popularity. Second, through manual inspection of 646 projects, we construct a taxonomy of 43 software-goal categories spanning conceptual design, datasets, system implementation, simulation, testing, and tools. Third, we conduct an in-depth analysis of projects with source code, revealing a highly heterogeneous and task-specialized ecosystem with 66 programming languages and diverse implementation strategies. Finally, we summarize key findings and derive actionable implications for satellite developers and researchers.

</details>


### [103] [Challenges and Contributions in Quality of AI-Based Software: A Systematic Mapping Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26215)
*Maryum Hamdani,Mateen Ahmed Abbasi,Marko Jäntti,Markku Tukiainen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过系统映射研究（SMS），综合分析了2020年1月至2026年1月间发表的33项关于AI软件质量的主要研究，识别出六大挑战类别，其中最突出的是现有质量评估模型的局限性、非功能性需求管理问题、质量感知开发和质量保证。研究呼吁研究人员、工业从业者与标准化组织合作，制定全面的质量评估及其测量方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着人工智能日益嵌入现代软件系统，其质量应如何定义、评估和保证成为亟待回答的重要问题。本文旨在系统性地梳理现有研究中关于AI软件质量的关键挑战和不足之处。

Method: 采用系统映射研究方法（SMS），对五个电子数据源进行自动化搜索、筛选和滚动式引文追踪，纳入2020年1月至2026年1月间发表的33项主要研究进行综合分析。

Result: 识别出六大反复出现的挑战类别，其中"现有质量评估模型的局限性"最为突出，其次是"非功能性需求管理"、"质量感知开发"和"质量保证"等方面的问题。

Conclusion: 研究指出需要研究人员、工业实践者与标准化组织之间的协同合作，以制定全面的AI软件质量评估体系及其配套测量方法。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI) is increasingly embedded in modern software systems, raising important questions about how its quality should be defined, assessed, and assured. This paper presents a Systematic Mapping Study (SMS) on the quality of AI-based software. The study synthesizes primary studies published between January 2020 and January 2026 and selected from five electronic data sources. A total of 33 primary studies were included after automated search, screening, and snowballing. The results identify six recurring challenge categories, with the most prominent being limitations in existing quality assessment models, followed by issues in non-functional requirement management, quality-aware development, and quality assurance. The findings suggest a call for collaboration of researchers and industrial practitioners with standardization organizations, that could possibly devise comprehensive quality assessments and their measurement methods.

</details>


### [104] [The Green Software Landscape: A Systematic Mapping Study on Evolution, Applications, Software Lifecycle, and Best Practices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26229)
*Max Hort,Maria Kechagia,Federica Sarro*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对2010至2024年间绿色软件工程（Green SE）领域的390篇文献进行了系统映射研究，发现SE会议是该领域文献的主要发表场所，2023年起研究数量激增且主要由AI相关文献驱动，优化研究和基准测试是最常见的研究类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能源消耗和气候变化使可持续性成为软件工程（SE）领域的核心议题，绿色软件工程（Green SE）应运而生。过去15年涌现了大量相关解决方案，但缺乏对该领域整体演化的系统性分析，因此需要通过系统映射研究来全面审视其发展脉络。

Method: 采用系统映射研究方法，收集了2010至2024年间发表的390篇绿色软件工程文献，按应用领域（如移动端、云端、AI）和研究类型（如优化研究、基准测试、文献综述）进行分类；另从代表性子集79篇论文中分析了能源测量实验中涉及的关键要素，包括硬件、测量方法、稳定性和可复现性。

Result: 研究发现：SE会议承载了大部分能源相关文献；2023年起绿色软件工程研究数量显著激增，主要由AI相关出版物驱动；优化研究和基准测试是最普遍的研究类型。

Conclusion: 本研究旨在帮助SE社区了解当前应对能源问题的各种方法，强调关键实验实践，并倡导继续推进更可持续的软件工程发展。

Abstract: Energy consumption and climate change have made sustainability critical in Software Engineering (SE), driving the emergence of Green SE. Over the past 15 years, numerous solutions for sustainable software systems have been published by the SE community, offering a rich resource for analyzing the field's evolution. To explore this, we conducted a systematic mapping study of Green SE research published between 2010 and 2024. We collected 390 publications, categorizing them by application domain (e.g., mobile, cloud, AI) and research type (e.g., optimisation study, benchmarking, literature review Additionally, we analyzed a representative subset of 79 papers to classify the key elements-such as hardware, measurement, stability, and replicability-considered during energy measurement experiments. Our findings indicate that SE conferences host the majority of energy-related literature. Notably, Green SE studies surged in popularity starting in 2023, largely driven by AI-related publications. Optimization and benchmarking emerged as the most prevalent research types. Ultimately, we aim to inform the SE community about current approaches to energy concerns, highlight critical experimental practices, and advocate for continued action toward more sustainable software engineering.

</details>


### [105] ["A Second Set of Eyes": The Process and Challenges of Software Documentation Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26232)
*Avinash Bhat,Ian Arawjo,Disha Shrivastava,Jin L. C. Guo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过对31位资深技术写作者的半结构化访谈，揭示了文档审查过程中五个不同阶段（自我审查、技术审查、编辑审查、试运行测试、发布后反馈），并指出文档审查是一个关键但研究不足的协作工作领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究已建立了"好文档"的质量标准，但尚未探讨实践者如何运用专业知识来改进文档质量，以及在此过程中面临的挑战。

Method: 对来自不同组织的31位资深技术写作者进行半结构化访谈，分析文档审查过程中的个人与协作努力。

Result: 识别出文档审查的五个不同阶段，每个阶段需要不同专业背景的实践者参与，以解决内容、呈现和用户体验方面的质量问题；同时发现了写作者在招募评审专家、协调开发时间线以及应对不适用于文档工作流程的工具等方面面临的组织和技术挑战。

Conclusion: 文档审查应被视为一个关键的协作工作场所，研究结果为流程改进和工具支持开辟了新的研究方向与设计可能性。

Abstract: Organizations assign documentation work to technical writers, yet the knowledge required to produce it is distributed across developers, managers, and other practitioners. Prior work has established quality criteria for judging "good" documentation, but it has not examined how practitioners bring that expertise to improve documentation quality or the challenges they face in doing so. Through semi-structured interviews with experienced technical writers ($n=31$) from different organizations, our work reveals the individual and collaborative effort required to maintain documentation quality. We identify five distinct stages of the documentation review process: self review, technical review, editorial review, play testing, and post-publication feedback. Each stage draws on practitioners with distinct expertise to address quality across content, presentation, and user experience. Our findings surface organizational and technical challenges writers face in recruiting expert reviewers, navigating development timelines, and contending with tools not specifically designed for documentation workflows. Our work positions documentation review as a crucial yet understudied site of collaborative work and opens new research and design directions for process improvement and tool support.

</details>


### [106] [When Review Alone No Longer Scales: Layered Supervision in AI-Assisted Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26316)
*Markus Stolze,Mirco Strässle*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过定性访谈研究，探讨AI辅助开发工具如何促使软件团队的"护栏"（质量保障机制）从传统的以代码审查为核心，演变为多层次监督体系，包括预防性护栏、可执行护栏和人工监督三个层面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助开发工具大幅提高了软件工程师的代码生成速度和数量，给传统的质量保障"护栏"（如代码审查、测试、CI/CD）带来了巨大压力，但目前对现有护栏如何应对这一变化的研究甚少。

Method: 采用定性访谈研究方法，对五位软件工程实践者进行深入访谈，并将其置于更广泛的从业者调查背景中进行分析。

Result: 研究发现组织将监督工作分布在多个护栏层：预防性护栏（将架构意图和惯例外部化为机器可解释形式）、可执行护栏（复用linting、测试、CI/CD作为可扩展的监督基础设施）、人工监督（从逐行审查转向以架构推理、可解释性和长期可维护性为重点的监督性解释）。

Conclusion: 研究将其描述为从以审查为中心的护栏向分层监督的转变，在这一转变中，没有任何单一护栏能独自承担全部监督负载，需要多层协作共同保障质量。

Abstract: AI-assisted development tools enable software engineers to generate implementations at substantially higher speed and volume than in traditional workflows. Software teams have long relied on guardrails -- standing control mechanisms such as code review, linting, testing, and CI/CD pipelines -- to maintain quality and coordination. High-throughput AI-assisted generation increases pressure on these guardrails -- straining their capacity to keep pace with the volume and rate of generated changes -- and reshapes how organizations supervise development workflows, yet relatively little is known about how existing guardrails evolve in response. We conducted a qualitative interview study with five software engineering practitioners, situated within a broader practitioner survey. Our findings indicate that organizations distribute the work of supervision across multiple guardrail layers: preventive guardrails (produced by externalizing architectural intent and conventions into machine-interpretable form), executable guardrails (linting, testing, and CI/CD repurposed as scalable supervision infrastructure), and human oversight (shifting from line-by-line inspection toward supervisory interpretation focused on architectural reasoning, explainability, and long-term maintainability). We characterize this as a transition from review- centric guardrails toward layered supervision, in which no single guardrail carries the supervision load alone.

</details>


### [107] [Investigating Software Aging in LLM-Generated Software Systems across Generation-and-Execution Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26391)
*Cesar Santos,Michele Vitagliano,Roberto Natella,Ermeson Andrade*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文实验性地研究了LLM生成的服务型应用在不同编程语言（JavaScript、Python、Rust）下的软件老化（software aging）症状。通过48小时负载执行，利用Mann-Kendall检验和Sen斜率估计器分析内存使用、响应时间和吞吐量，发现内存使用是最一致的老化指标，并辅以静态分析和与人工编写实现的对比验证了结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注LLM生成代码的功能正确性、安全性、可维护性和健壮性，但对这些系统在持续运行条件下的长期可靠性知之甚少。功能正确性不足以评估软件在实际部署环境中的运行可靠性。

Method: 基于BaxBench的后端场景，使用LLM生成平台生成JavaScript、Python和Rust三种语言的应用程序，通过BaxBench派生的测试进行验证，并执行48小时工作负载测试。监控内存使用、响应时间和吞吐量，使用Mann-Kendall检验和Sen斜率估计器进行趋势分析，同时结合生成代码的静态分析和与人工编写实现的探索性对比。

Result: 内存使用是软件老化最一致的指标，在大多数应用-语言组合中呈现统计显著的上升趋势；响应时间和吞吐量表现出更异质的行为。静态分析识别出可能的代码级老化机制，与人工编写系统的对比显示老化趋势也可能出现在手动开发的实现中。

Conclusion: 功能正确性不足以评估LLM生成软件在持续运行环境中的运行可靠性，软件老化分析应作为部署前评估的重要组成部分。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used to generate executable software systems from natural language specifications, accelerating development and reducing manual implementation effort. Although recent studies have investigated the functional correctness, security, maintainability, and robustness of LLM-generated code, little is known about the long-term reliability of such systems under sustained execution. In this paper, we experimentally investigate software aging symptoms in LLM-generated service-based applications across different programming languages. Using backend scenarios derived from BaxBench, we generated applications targeting JavaScript, Python, and Rust through LLM-based generation platforms, validated them with BaxBench-derived tests, and subjected them to 48-hour workload executions. We monitored memory usage, response time, and throughput and analyzed them using the Mann--Kendall test and Sen's slope estimator. We further complemented the runtime evaluation with static analysis of the generated source code and an exploratory comparison with human-written implementations of related backend scenarios. The results show that memory usage is the most consistent indicator of potential software aging, with statistically significant upward trends in most application-language combinations, while response time and throughput exhibit more heterogeneous behavior. Static analysis identified plausible code-level aging mechanisms, and the comparison with human-written systems showed that aging trends can also emerge in manually developed implementations. These findings indicate that functional correctness alone is insufficient to assess the operational reliability of LLM-generated software before deployment in continuously running environments.

</details>


### [108] [Spec2Vision: Contract-Guided Delivery of AI-Generated Computer Vision Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26400)
*Ghfran Jabour,Sergey Ivanov*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Spec2Vision是一个面向计算机视觉代码生成的研究框架，通过分阶段运行时（合成、筛选、测试和有界修复）将任务契约显式化，在17个CV任务、10个可执行条件和850次主运行上达到81/85评估器测试通过率，显著优于基线（最高35/85），证明了任务契约的显式保持是CV代码生成成功的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成的计算机视觉代码即使可运行，也可能不满足下游评估器强制执行的任务契约。现有方法忽略了"可运行"与"交付成功"之间的鸿沟，需要一种能显式保持任务契约的生成与评估框架。

Method: 提出Spec2Vision框架，采用分阶段运行时管线，在合成、筛选、测试及有界修复各阶段均保持任务契约显式化。基准包含17个CV任务、10个可执行条件和每个任务-条件单元5次重复共850次主运行，并通过消融实验（移除结构修复、兼容性脚手架、生成器预检）及多级可执行单智能体基线进行对比评估。

Result: Spec2Vision在主评估中达到81/85评估器测试通过；移除结构修复降至55/85，移除兼容性脚手架降至58/85，移除生成器预检降至39/85。最佳单智能体基线（直接暴露源码规范）仅达到35/85，而轻量级基线虽然85/85核心可运行，但仅17/85评估器通过且6/85严格交付成功。

Conclusion: 可运行性不等于交付成功；最强的证据表明，在分阶段生成、检查和修复过程中保持任务契约显式化，是提升CV代码生成评测通过率的核心因素。项目提供了完整的可审计工件。

Abstract: Generated computer-vision code can be runnable without satisfying the task contract enforced by a downstream evaluator. We study that gap with Spec2Vision, an experimental framework for producing and evaluating specification-grounded CV pipeline bundles through a staged runtime that keeps the task contract explicit across synthesis, screening, testing, and bounded repair. The benchmark evaluates 17 CV tasks, 10 executable conditions, and 5 repeats per task-condition cell, for 850 primary runs. In the primary 850-run evaluation, Spec2Vision reaches 81/85 evaluator-test passes; removing structural repair drops to 55/85, compatibility scaffolding to 58/85, and generator preflight to 39/85. The executable single-agent baselines expose progressively richer task specifications to the model, culminating in direct source-spec exposure, yet remain much weaker overall, from 17/85 for lightweight task grounding to 35/85 evaluator-test passes. The lightweight baseline nevertheless remains core-runnable in 85/85 runs but reaches only 17/85 evaluator-test passes and 6/85 strict-delivery successes, showing that runnability is not equivalent to delivery. Across this benchmark, the strongest evidence comes from keeping the task contract explicit across staged generation, checking, and repair. Artifacts are provided to support audit of run bundles, model-visible inputs, and derived tables.

</details>


### [109] [STILL: Recovering Lowered STL Semantics for LLM-assisted C++ Decompilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26408)
*Xiaohan Wang,Kevin Leach*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出STILL，一种从剥离控制流图中预测STL容器语义并生成提示信息以辅助LLM反编译的结构化语义接口，在StlBench和HumanEval基准上显著提升了可执行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM辅助反编译虽能提升代码可读性和可重执行性，但在处理使用标准模板库（STL）的剥离C++函数时仍表现不佳。编译、优化和符号剥离会丢失或模糊容器类型和库调用结构等源码级语义，而传统反编译器输出往往无法恢复这些信息。

Method: STILL从剥离的控制流图中预测函数级别的STL容器语义，并将其渲染为紧凑的提示信息供LLM精炼使用。该接口作为结构化语义层连接底层控制流图与上层LLM反编译过程。

Result: 在StlBench上，STILL能预测常见的容器级STL语义，跨数据集结果在稳定字符串和向量切片上表现最强。在剥离的HumanEval反编译任务中，STILL提示使DeepSeek-chat精炼达到28.4%的可执行性，显著高于无提示精炼的17.4%和原始Ghidra反编译的8.9%。提示效用依赖于下游基础模型，反编译专用模型需轻量适配才能受益。

Conclusion: STILL通过提供结构化语义提示有效提升了LLM对剥离STL C++函数的反编译质量，但提示的收益与下游基础模型的选择密切相关，需要针对不同模型进行适配。

Abstract: LLM-assisted decompilation improves readability and re-executability, but still underperforms on stripped C++ functions that use the Standard Template Library (STL). Compilation, optimization, and symbol stripping remove or obscure source-level semantics such as container types and library-call structure, while traditional decompiler output often fails to recover them. We present STILL, a structured semantic interface that predicts function-level STL container semantics from stripped control-flow graphs and renders them as compact hints for LLM refinement. On StlBench, STILL predicts common container-level STL semantics, with the strongest cross-dataset results for stable string and vector slices. On stripped HumanEval decompilation, these hints enable DeepSeek-chat refinement to reach 28.4% executability, compared with 17.4% for no-hint refinement and 8.9% for raw Ghidra decompilation; hint utility is downstream-backbone-dependent, with decompilation-specialized models requiring lightweight adaptation to benefit from the same interface.

</details>


### [110] [DeepRepro: State-Aware Subplanning for Paper-to-Code Reproduction in Evolving Repositories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26557)
*Hongru Song,Ruqing Zhang,Jiafeng Guo,Xueqi Cheng,Maarten de Rijke*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: DeepRepro是一个基于执行状态感知子规划的状态感知框架，用于ML论文到代码的自动复现，通过动态将仓库状态和运行时反馈转化为细粒度子计划，在PaperBench Code-Dev上优于现有科学和商业代码代理基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的论文到代码复现系统通常依赖静态的前期规划然后顺序生成文件级代码，但随着依赖、接口和执行反馈的不断变化，这种方法容易导致不一致性问题。

Method: DeepRepro采用执行状态感知的子规划机制，动态地将演化的仓库状态和运行时反馈转化为细粒度的实现子计划，使规划在整个仓库构建过程中与执行保持对齐；同时结合仓库感知的编排框架和轻量级过程感知接口，用于长视距复现过程的透明监控。

Result: 在PaperBench Code-Dev基准上，DeepRepro持续优于多个强大的科学和商业代码代理基线。

Conclusion: 状态感知的动态子规划方法能有效解决ML论文到代码复现中因仓库状态持续演化导致的规划与执行不一致问题，为长视距软件工程自动化提供了新的可行路径。

Abstract: Recent advances in agentic large language models (LLMs) have enabled increasingly autonomous software engineering workflows, yet automatic machine learning (ML) paper-to-code reproduction remains a challenging long-horizon problem. Unlike conventional code generation, this task requires constructing and maintaining a fully functional repository whose state continuously evolves during execution. Existing systems typically rely on static upfront planning followed by sequential file-level generation, which often leads to inconsistencies as dependencies, interfaces, and execution feedback change over time. We propose DeepRepro, a state-aware framework for paper-to-code reproduction based on execution-state-aware subplanning. DeepRepro dynamically transforms evolving repository states and runtime feedback into fine-grained implementation subplans, keeping planning aligned with execution throughout repository construction. The framework further incorporates repository-aware orchestration and a lightweight process-aware interface for transparent monitoring of long-horizon reproduction. Experiments on PaperBench Code-Dev show that DeepRepro consistently outperforms strong scientific and commercial code-agent baselines.

</details>


### [111] [The Thousand-Graph Hypothesis: A Testable Hypothesis of Task-Conditioned Relation Materialization in Repository-Level Code Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26602)
*Fei Ding*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出了一种仅实体外部接口与任务条件化关系物化方法，采用两层索引（全局路由+局部实体聚焦），在零预构建实体关系边的条件下，SWE-bench Verified上两层条件达到95.6%成功率，优于基础（92.1%）和单层（94.2%）基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型软件仓库常超出模型上下文限制，将仓库知识训练进模型成本高且易过时；本地检索可能遗漏分散需求；显式关系图需持续维护负担。现有方案难以兼顾成本、时效与覆盖率。

Method: 提出仅实体（entity-only）外部接口，在推理时进行任务条件化的关系物化（task-conditioned relation materialization）。采用两层索引结构：第一层负责全局路由，第二层聚焦局部实体。在DeepSeek-V4-Flash和SWE-bench Verified上评估。

Result: 零预构建实体关系边条件下，基础、单层、两层条件分别达到92.1%、94.2%、95.6%成功率，验证了两层索引随层次增加性能持续提升。

Conclusion: 该方案通过消除预构建关系边依赖，在推理期动态物化关系，有效降低维护成本的同时提升代码仓库级任务成功率。

Abstract: Large software repositories are often beyond model context limits. Training repository knowledge into models is costly and quickly stale, while local retrieval can miss scattered requirements, and explicit relation graphs add ongoing maintenance burden. We propose an entity-only external interface with task-conditioned relation materialization during inference. A two-layer index separates global routing from local entity focus and is evaluated on DeepSeek-V4-Flash and SWE-bench Verified. The base, one-layer, and two-layer conditions achieve 92.1%, 94.2%, and 95.6% success, respectively, under zero pre-built entity-relation edges.

</details>


### [112] [Processing/p5 Defined through Practice and Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26614)
*Kit Kuksenok,Lee Tusman*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过在不同编程语言（JavaScript和Lua）中实现Processing/p5库的案例研究，提出了一套跨语言定义Processing/p5框架的软件决策指导原则，旨在为创意编程和算法艺术领域的库开发提供参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Processing/p5作为创意编程工具在不同语言中具有一致的优先级和设计理念，但目前缺乏对其核心特征的系统性提炼。作者希望通过跨语言实现案例，识别出构成Processing/p5本质的决策要素，从而促进学习、研究和艺术实践。

Method: 采用案例研究方法，在JavaScript和Lua两种宿主语言中分别实现Processing/p5库，通过对比分析提取出跨语言共享的软件决策指导原则，并与其他探索性和创意工具的决策进行讨论对比。

Result: 提出了一套构成Processing/p5的软件决策指导方面列表，无论宿主语言如何，这些方面都保持一致。并通过与现有工具的对比，展示了每个指导方面可以有不同的操作化方式。

Conclusion: 该框架揭示了Processing/p5在不同语言生态中的核心一致性，为新创意编码库的创建提供了指导，有助于推动学习、研究和艺术实践领域的发展。

Abstract: Processing/p5 libraries across different programming languages enact consistent priorities for creative coding as a designed experience. While different programming language ecosystems, like Java and JavaScript, are each associated with their own affordances, community norms, and patterns of use, Processing/p5 sketches across these languages share similarities. Based on case studies of building an implementation of Processing/p5 in two host languages, JavaScript and Lua, we propose a list of software decision-making guiding aspects that constitute Processing/p5, regardless of host language. We discuss this framework in the context of decisions in other exploratory and creative tools that demonstrate how each of the guiding aspects can be operationalized differently than in the case studies. The proposed list highlights opportunities for learning, research, and artistic practice through creation of new Processing/p5 libraries for creative coding and algorithmic art.

</details>


### [113] [FaultLens: Learning Compact Behavioral Test Suites for Generated Operational Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26746)
*Zeming Liu,Hang Lyu,Jingtao Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: FaultLens是一种从已执行证据中学习紧凑行为测试套件的方法，通过故障驱动与多样性驱动的混合策略，用极小的测试预算覆盖绝大多数可动态杀死的故障，并在下游部署中消除严重尾部回归。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成的操作程序通常用少量手写示例或详尽回归套件验证，前者可能遗漏稀疏边界和交互故障，后者则成本过高。需要在测试有效性与执行成本之间取得平衡，同时保留与已执行证据的可审计关联。

Method: FaultLens首先执行一次丰富的探针域，将故障-探针杀死关系存储为稀疏结果缓存；仅从早期程序代中学习探针排序。采用交替混合策略：故障驱动贪婪组件利用已知杀死结构，变异无关的多样性组件覆盖探针族、案例、模板和时间分布。

Result: 在20个生成操作策略、4个环境、1200个运行摘要和420万个程序-探针对上评估：32探针混合套件覆盖99.0%可动态杀死故障，仅用穷举域的1.2-2.0%；当整个故障族从训练中剔除时，多样性策略将场景族宏覆盖从84.6%提升至94.9%；保守准入规则将严重尾部回归从15/20降至0/20。

Conclusion: FaultLens提供了一种优先化的证据机制而非正确性证明，明确公开其预算、证据来源、泛化划分和遗漏情况，在显著降低测试成本的同时保持对新故障机制的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Generated operational programs are often validated with either a few hand-written examples or exhaustive regression suites. The former can miss sparse boundary and interaction faults, while the latter can be unnecessarily expensive. We introduce FaultLens, a method for learning compact behavioral test suites while preserving an auditable connection to executed evidence. It executes a rich probe domain once, stores the fault-probe kill relation as a sparse outcome cache, and learns probe orderings only from earlier program generations. A fault-driven greedy component exploits known kill structure, while a mutation-independent diversity component covers probe families, cases, templates, and temporal bins. Their alternating hybrid remains useful when a new program contains a fault mechanism absent from ordering construction.
  We evaluate twenty generated operational policies across four environments, ten execution seeds, 1,200 measured run summaries, 2,160 controlled program transformations, and 4,120,200 executed program-probe pairs. Of 1,960 intended faulty transformations, 1,779 alter a contract or output somewhere in the finite audit domain; 200 additional controls preserve behavior. A 32-probe hybrid learned on generations 1-3 covers 576/582 (99.0%) dynamically killable faults in generations 4-5 using 1.2-2.0% of the exhaustive domain. With an entire fault family withheld from training, diversity raises scenario-family macro coverage from 84.6% to 94.9%. In a downstream deployment study, a conservative admission rule reduces severe tail regressions from 15/20 program-environment groups to 0/20. FaultLens provides a prioritized evidence mechanism, not a proof of correctness, and makes its budget, evidence source, generalization split, and misses explicit.

</details>


### [114] [Beyond Execution: Auditing Experimental Fidelity in LLM-Driven Scientific Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26753)
*Lezhi Yu,Xiaogang Xu,Yuhua Zhou,Shuibing He,Aimin Pan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出ABE-Ralph，一个参考锚定的审计框架，用于检测LLM智能体在科学实验中产生的方法论幻觉（如暗中缩减数据、用查表函数替代学习组件等）。在30个长时程复现任务和23个NatureBench发现任务中分别达到93%的稳健执行率并匹配/超越SOTA，揭示AI科学家评估必须检验实验设计是否忠实检验论文主张。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM科学实验智能体不仅需要生成可执行代码，还必须忠实实现参考方法、设计能检验论文主张的实验并提供支持证据。然而现有智能体经常产生方法论幻觉——暗中缩减数据集或训练预算、用查表/神谕函数替换失败组件、在受限设置下得出方法优势消失的结论，这些失败难以被发现。

Method: ABE-Ralph框架将声明、协议、必要组件、基线和指标表示为结构化实验约束，通过8步工作流引导实现，并执行定量、定性和代码级三重验证，以检测方法论幻觉。

Result: 在覆盖12个机器学习领域的30个长时程复现运行中，ABE-Ralph达到93%的稳健执行率，识别出5种科学失败模式；在23个NatureBench发现任务中，ABE-Ralph在5个任务上匹配或超越现有最优性能。

Conclusion: 可靠的AI科学家评估必须验证实验设计是否忠实检验了预期主张、证据是否支持该主张，而非仅将代码执行成功或看似合理的指标视为科学成功的证据。

Abstract: LLM agents used for scientific experimentation must do more than generate executable code: they must implement the reference method faithfully, design experiments that test the paper's claims, and provide evidence supporting those claims. We show that agents often produce methodological hallucinations: silently reducing datasets or training budgets, replacing failed learning or generative components with lookup or oracle functions, or drawing conclusions from resource-limited settings where a method's claimed advantage disappears. To detect these failures, we introduce ABE-Ralph, a reference-anchored auditing framework that represents claims, protocols, required components, baselines, and metrics as structured experimental constraints, guides implementation through an 8-step workflow, and performs quantitative, qualitative, and code-level verification. Across 30 long-horizon reproduction runs covering 12 machine learning domains, ABE-Ralph achieves a 93% robust execution rate and identifies five scientific failure modes. In 23 NatureBench discovery tasks, ABE-Ralph matches or exceeds state-of-the-art performance on 5 tasks. These results show that reliable evaluation of AI scientists must assess whether the experimental design faithfully tests the intended claim and whether the resulting evidence supports it, rather than treating code execution or plausible metrics as evidence of scientific success.

</details>


### [115] [An Empirical Evaluation of Using Large Language Models for Automated Model-Based Test Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27094)
*Hafize Sanli,Onur Kilincceker,Cihat Cetinkaya*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对五种最新大语言模型（GPT-5.1、GPT-5.2、Claude Opus 4.5、Claude Sonnet 4.5和Gemini 2.5 Pro）在自动化模型驱动测试生成方面的能力进行了实证评估，并与先进的MBT工具GraphWalker及其内置算法进行了对比，覆盖四个复杂度和应用领域各异的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型驱动测试（MBT）在工业落地中面临可扩展性挑战，需要探索新的方法来优化和缩短测试路径及步长，提高MBT的工业适用性。

Method: 采用实证评估方法，将五种SOTA LLM与GraphWalker及其内置算法（随机和快速随机，针对边覆盖和顶点覆盖设置）进行对比测试，使用四个GraphWalker模型（两个Web应用Parabank和Testinium，两个硬件应用TLC和RISC-V），覆盖不同复杂度层级。

Result: 评估结果表明LLM在优化和缩短测试路径及步长方面展现出强大潜力，相较于传统MBT工具具有明显优势。

Conclusion: LLM在自动化模型驱动测试生成领域具有显著前景，能够有效提升MBT的可扩展性，为工业应用提供更优化的测试路径方案。

Abstract: Large language models have shown strong potential for software engineering tasks, particularly software testing. Model-based testing (MBT) is a software testing technique. To address the broad scalability challenge for industrial adoption of MBTs, our paper presents an empirical evaluation of Large Language Models (LLMs) for automated model-based test generation, compared with a state-of-the-art model-based testing tool (GraphWalker) and its built-in algorithms (random and quick random for edge and vertex coverage settings). Our evaluation indicates strong potential to optimize and shorten test paths and step sizes using the recent five state-of-the-art LLMs (GPT-5.1, GPT-5.2, Claude Opus 4.5, Claude Sonnet 4.5, and Gemini 2.5 Pro) against four GraphWalker models (two web applications (Parabank and Testinium) and two hardware applications (TLC and RISC-V) ) of escalating complexity.

</details>


### [116] [Mutation Testing for Reproducibility Safeguards in Machine Learning Research Software: An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27100)
*Ilya Shulepov*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究机器学习研究仓库中现有验证工作流对可复现性相关变更的检测能力。通过MLReproMutate工具对39个冻结仓库案例施加四类变异（随机种子、依赖固定、数据划分、交叉验证折数），结果发现现有验证工作流仅检测出23个确认非等价变异中的2个（检测比例8.7%），表明现有验证工作流往往难以捕捉可复现性相关的重要实验选择变更。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习研究的可复现性取决于随机种子、依赖版本、数据划分和评估配置等实验选择，然而现有的仓库验证工作流即使执行成功也可能无法检测到这些关键选择的变更，因此需要研究现有验证工作流对可复现性相关变更的检测能力。

Method: 开发MLReproMutate工具，对ML研究仓库施加受控的可复现性相关变异，并针对仓库中已有的验证工作流进行评估。采用结果盲法（outcome-blind）实验设计，对39个冻结仓库操作案例应用四类变异：随机种子、依赖固定、数据划分和交叉验证折数。在观察变异结果前预先固定仓库修订版本、变异候选和验证工作流，并通过有界恢复程序扩大可评估案例集。

Result: 39个案例中初次执行获得13个结果，经有界恢复程序后合并可评估集增至24个。排除1个确认等价变异后，剩余23个确认非等价变异中，现有验证工作流仅检测出2个，观测检测比例为8.7%。

Conclusion: 研究结果并不意味着对应仓库不可复现，而是表明在样本中现有验证工作流常常无法检测特定受控的可复现性相关变更。这为将可复现性导向的变异测试作为互补手段提供了动机，用以评估研究软件保障措施是否有效约束实验上重要的选择。

Abstract: Reproducibility in machine-learning research depends on experimental choices such as random seeds, dependency versions, data partitioning, and evaluation configuration. Existing repository validation workflows may execute successfully without detecting changes to such choices. We study this problem using MLReproMutate, research software that applies controlled, reproducibility-relevant mutations to ML research repositories and evaluates them against validation workflows already present in those repositories.
  We conducted an outcome-blind empirical study of 39 frozen repository-operator cases using four mutation classes: random seed, dependency pin, data split, and cross-validation fold count. Repository revisions, mutation candidates, and validation workflows were fixed before mutation outcomes were observed. Primary execution yielded outcomes for 13 of 39 cases; a bounded restoration procedure increased the combined evaluable set to 24. After excluding one confirmed-equivalent mutation, 23 confirmed non-equivalent mutations remained. The selected validation workflows detected 2 of these 23 mutations, corresponding to an observed detection proportion of 8.7%.
  These results do not imply that the corresponding repositories are irreproducible. Rather, they show that, in this sample, existing validation workflows often did not detect the particular controlled reproducibility-relevant changes introduced by the study. The findings motivate reproducibility-oriented mutation testing as a complementary way to assess whether research-software safeguards constrain experimentally important choices.

</details>


### [117] [AROMA+: A Study of Factors Affecting Reproducible Builds in the Maven Ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27125)
*Mehdi Keshani,Amirhossein Rahmati,Mohammad Hossein Aref,Abbas Heydarnoori*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了AROMA+工具，用于自动化地在Maven生态系统中查找库的源代码和原始构建环境信息，以支持可复现构建。该工具在字段级对比上与现有手工方法达到99.8%的准确率，对Maven Central上32%的包实现自动复现可行性，其中12%完全可复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件供应链中存在安全风险，可复现构建是重要的缓解策略。虽然Debian社区已有大量可复现性研究，但作为Java供应链核心的Maven生态系统的可复现性研究相对不足。Reproducible Central项目虽然维护了可复现Maven库的清单，但依赖人工维护，效率低下且容易出错。

Method: 开发AROMA+工具，通过多种启发式方法从Maven制品和源代码仓库中自动获取关键信息（包括源代码位置和原始构建环境），利用这些信息对Maven Central上的包进行复现尝试，并与现有的手工维护方法进行字段级对比验证。

Result: AROMA+在与现有手工方法进行字段级对比时达到99.8%的准确率；发现手工维护清单中的缺陷（如断裂的仓库链接）；在Maven Central上32%的包可自动复现，其中12%完全可复现；成功复现了新包并贡献至Reproducible Central仓库。

Conclusion: 自动化工具在Maven生态系统的可复现性研究中表现出高准确率和实用性，不仅验证了自动复现的可行性，还能发现人工维护中的错误。研究提供了可操作的建议并公开了数据集和工具，为该领域的进一步工作奠定了基础。

Abstract: Modern software engineering establishes software supply chains and relies on tools and libraries to improve productivity. However, reusing external software in a project presents a security risk when the source of the component is unknown or the consistency of a component cannot be verified. Reproducible builds present a mitigation strategy, as they can confirm the origin and consistency of reused components. A large reproducibility community has formed for Debian, but the reproducibility of the Maven ecosystem, the backbone of the Java supply chain, remains understudied in comparison. Reproducible Central is an initiative that curates a list of reproducible Maven libraries, but the list is limited and challenging to maintain due to manual efforts. Our research aims to support these efforts in the Maven ecosystem through automation. We investigate the feasibility of automatically finding the source code of a library from its Maven release and recovering information about the original release environment. Our tool, AROMA+, can obtain this critical information from the artifact and the source repository through several heuristics and we use the results for reproduction attempts of packages on Maven Central. Overall, our approach achieves an accuracy of up to 99.8% when compared field-by-field to the existing manual approach. In some instances, we even detected flaws in the manually maintained list, such as broken repository links. We reveal that automatic reproducibility is feasible for 32% of the packages on Maven Central using AROMA+, and 12% of these packages are fully reproducible. We demonstrate our ability to successfully reproduce new packages and have contributed some of them to the Reproducible Central repository. Additionally, we highlight actionable insights, outline future work in this area, and make our dataset and tools publicly available.

</details>


### [118] [AgentDV: Closed-Loop Agentic AI for Hardware Design Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27148)
*Navya Goli,Junzhe Liu,Zhenge Jia,Umamaheswara Rao Tida*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍AgentDV，一个用于自动化RTL验证环境生成的闭环智能体AI框架。它通过工具接地验证流水线（结合LLM分析、测试台构建、仿真、覆盖率测量和迭代优化），将单次LLM测试台生成转变为迭代流程。核心贡献包括可运行性过滤、CSR接地检查、覆盖率引导迭代三个关键机制。实验表明AgentDV在多个DUT和开源IP上显著优于直接单次提示方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RTL验证占SoC开发工作量的主要部分，但现有LLM生成的验证代码往往不可运行、与设计不一致且无法产生覆盖率。直接单次提示（single-shot）生成测试台的成功率极低，实际应用价值有限。

Method: AgentDV采用闭环智能体框架，包含三个关键机制：(1)可运行性过滤（runnability filtering）——拒绝无效生成的验证环境；(2)CSR接地检查（CSR-grounded checking）——减少幻觉信号和不正确的预期行为；(3)覆盖率引导迭代（coverage-guided iteration）——基于测量到的验证差距重新生成测试。框架将LLM引导分析、测试台构建、仿真、覆盖率测量和迭代优化整合为一个流水线。

Result: 在四个DUT上实现100%通过率，所有DUT平均通过率为80.9%（Claude Sonnet 4.6）；Llama和Qwen模型分别获得58.7%和60.6%的平均通过率。行覆盖率分别为74.5%、69.1%和64.9%，分支覆盖率为88.4%、82.3%和76.7%。直接单次提示在基准测试上无法产生有效的覆盖率环境。

Conclusion: AgentDV证明了将LLM生成与工具接地验证流程结合，可以显著提升RTL测试台生成的质量和可用性。该方法在不同LLM上均展现有效性，为自动化RTL验证环境生成提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Register-transfer level (RTL) verification consumes a major part of modern system-on-chip (SoC) development effort. Yet, recent LLM-based verification-code generation often fails to produce runnable, design-consistent, and coverage-producing testbenches. We present AgentDV, a closed-loop agentic AI framework for automated RTL verification environment generation. AgentDV transforms single-shot LLM testbench generation into a tool-grounded verification pipeline by combining LLM-guided analysis, testbench construction, simulation, coverage measurement, and iterative refinement. The framework introduces three key ideas: 1) runnability filtering to reject invalid generated environments, 2) CSR-grounded checking to reduce hallucinated signals and incorrect expected behavior, and 3) coverage-guided iteration to regenerate tests based on measured verification gaps. We evaluate AgentDV using three LLMs on challenge DUTs and public OpenTitan peripheral and security IP blocks. From our analysis, we observed that direct single-shot prompting fails to produce a valid coverage-producing environment on benchmarks. AgentDV achieves 100% pass rate on four DUTs and an average of 80.9% pass rate on all DUTs using Claude Sonnet 4.6. Similarly, an average of 58.7% and 60.6% pass rate is achieved for Llama and Qwen models, respectively. In addition, an average of 74.5%, 69.1%, and 64.9% of line coverage and 88.4%, 82.3%, and 76.7% of branch coverage for the benchmarks under consideration for Claude Sonnet 4.6, Llama, and Qwen models, respectively.

</details>


### [119] [A Trans-Domain Digital Twin for Bio-Aware Control of Climate and Energy in Cattle Fattening Barns Using Single-Episode Optimizer Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27185)
*Mansoorali Amiri*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向封闭肉牛育肥舍的跨域数字孪生框架，具备单次学习能力，通过集成机理气候模拟器、牲畜生长模拟器、模型预测控制、轻量级强化学习和结构化知识记忆，在多头速率时间循环架构中实现生物感知的气候与能量协同控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 封闭式肉牛育肥舍中，室内气候与牛群生长相互耦合：气候环境影响饲料摄入、代谢产热和增重，而体重增长又会改变牛舍未来的热湿负荷及通风供热需求，亟需一种能够跨域联动气候、生长、能量与生物控制的框架。

Method: 提出跨域数字孪生框架，包含四个核心组件（机理气候模拟器、牲畜生长模拟器、模型预测控制、轻量级强化学习）和结构化知识记忆，采用多头速率时间循环架构：快时间环以5分钟为周期评估执行器决策，维持短期热舒适、安全与能效；慢时间环基于每日气候条件、饲料效率、产热和生长限制因素提供生物指导。

Result: 结果表明，气候、生长、能量、饲料、生物指导和记忆能够在单一可执行控制周期内实现联动。局限性包括需要田间验证、饲料压力管理改善以及减少执行器指令突变。

Conclusion: 该框架初步验证了跨域数字孪生在肉牛育肥舍生物感知气候与能量控制中的可行性，但仍需进行实地验证并进一步优化控制的稳定性与饲喂管理策略。

Abstract: In closed cattle-fattening barns, the indoor climate and herd growth are mutually interdependent. Temperature, relative humidity, airflow, and ventilation affect thermal comfort, feed intake, metabolic heat production, daily growth, feed efficiency, and energy consumption, while body-weight gain alters the future heat and moisture loads of the barn and, consequently, its ventilation, heating, and energy requirements. This article proposes a trans-domain digital twin framework with single-episode learning capability, customized for bio-aware climate and energy control in a closed cattle-fattening barn. The framework integrates a mechanistic climate simulator, a livestock growth simulator, model predictive control, lightweight reinforcement learning, and structured knowledge memory within a multi-rate temporal-loop architecture. The fast temporal loop operates every five minutes to evaluate actuator decisions and maintain short-term thermal comfort, safety, and energy efficiency, whereas the slow temporal loop provides biological guidance based on daily climatic conditions, feed efficiency, heat production, and growth-limiting factors. The results show that climate, growth, energy, feed, biological guidance, and memory can be linked within a single executable control cycle. Remaining limitations include the need for field validation, improved management of feed pressure, and reduction of abrupt actuator-command variations.

</details>


### [120] [Twelve Quick Tips for Managing IT Disasters in Small Research Software Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27196)
*Greg Wilson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文为小型研究软件团队提供了一份灾难规划与恢复的实用指南。文章指出2025年美国政府对科研团队的攻击、GitHub可用性下降、多国野火等事件凸显了研究计算系统的脆弱性。指南假设读者是在本职工作之外自行处理一切、且非经验丰富的系统管理员，同时建议利用科研机构中现有的研究计算组、数据管理员和环境健康安全办公室等专业资源来协助实现这些规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2025年发生的一系列事件（美国政府攻击科研团队、GitHub可用性首次跌破90%、多国野火迫使研究人员离开家园和实验室）揭示了研究计算系统的脆弱性。作者希望通过提供一份简明的灾难规划指南，帮助小型研究软件团队意识到灾前规划是预防灾难最有效的方式之一。

Method: 本文以短文指南的形式呈现，内容针对小型研究软件团队，设定前提为成员需在常规工作之外自行承担所有事务且不具备资深系统管理经验。文中提供具体的灾难规划和恢复建议，并指明哪些建议需要专业知识，同时引导读者利用机构内已有的专业支持资源（如研究计算组、数据管理员、环境健康与安全办公室）。

Result: 论文给出了灾难规划和恢复的具体操作指南，明确了"完成"的标准样态，并将其与机构内可借助的专业支持资源相对应，使非专业系统管理员也能找到实现路径。

Conclusion: 灾难规划是预防研究计算系统灾难最有效的手段之一。小型研究软件团队即使在资源有限、经验不足的情况下，通过遵循这份指南并善用机构内已有的专业支持资源，也能够建立起有效的灾难规划和恢复机制。

Abstract: In 2025, the US government launched an unprecedented series of attacks on its own scientific research groups. A year later GitHub dropped below 90% availability for the first time, while wildfires in Canada, France, Spain, and elsewhere forced researchers from the homes and labs. These events and others have reminded us just how fragile research computing systems can be, and that planning for disasters is one of the most effective ways to prevent them.
  This paper is a short guide to disaster planning and recovery for a small research software team. The tips assume you are doing everything yourself on top of your regular job, and that you aren't an experienced system administrator. Some of the tips do require that kind of expertise, but most research institutions have research computing groups, data librarians, and environmental health-and-safety offices whose entire job is to help with exactly these problems. This paper tells you what "done" looks like; they can often provide it.

</details>
